<a name="user-content-key-features"></a>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Key Features</h2><a id="user-content-key-features" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Key Features" href="#key-features"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Supports both client and server side of HTTP protocol.</li>
<li>Supports both client and server Web-Sockets out-of-the-box and avoids
Callback Hell.</li>
<li>Provides Web-server with middleware and pluggable routing.</li>
</ul>
<a name="user-content-getting-started"></a>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Getting started</h2><a id="user-content-getting-started" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Getting started" href="#getting-started"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<a name="user-content-client"></a>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Client</h3><a id="user-content-client" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Client" href="#client"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">To get something from the web:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto"><pre><span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-s1">aiohttp</span>
<span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-s1">asyncio</span>

<span class="pl-k">async</span> <span class="pl-k">def</span> <span class="pl-en">main</span>():

    <span class="pl-k">async</span> <span class="pl-k">with</span> <span class="pl-s1">aiohttp</span>.<span class="pl-c1">ClientSession</span>() <span class="pl-k">as</span> <span class="pl-s1">session</span>:
        <span class="pl-k">async</span> <span class="pl-k">with</span> <span class="pl-s1">session</span>.<span class="pl-c1">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">'https://python.org'</span>) <span class="pl-k">as</span> <span class="pl-s1">response</span>:

            <span class="pl-en">print</span>(<span class="pl-s">"Status:"</span>, <span class="pl-s1">response</span>.<span class="pl-c1">status</span>)
            <span class="pl-en">print</span>(<span class="pl-s">"Content-type:"</span>, <span class="pl-s1">response</span>.<span class="pl-c1">headers</span>[<span class="pl-s">'content-type'</span>])

            <span class="pl-s1">html</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-k">await</span> <span class="pl-s1">response</span>.<span class="pl-c1">text</span>()
            <span class="pl-en">print</span>(<span class="pl-s">"Body:"</span>, <span class="pl-s1">html</span>[:<span class="pl-c1">15</span>], <span class="pl-s">"..."</span>)

<span class="pl-s1">asyncio</span>.<span class="pl-c1">run</span>(<span class="pl-en">main</span>())</pre><div class="zeroclipboard-container">
    <clipboard-copy aria-label="Copy code to clipboard" class="ClipboardButton btn btn-invisible js-clipboard-copy m-2 p-0 d-flex flex-justify-center flex-items-center" data-copy-feedback="Copied!" data-tooltip-direction="w" value="import aiohttp
import asyncio

async def main():

    async with aiohttp.ClientSession() as session:
        async with session.get('https://python.org') as response:

            print(&quot;Status:&quot;, response.status)
            print(&quot;Content-type:&quot;, response.headers['content-type'])

            html = await response.text()
            print(&quot;Body:&quot;, html[:15], &quot;...&quot;)

<p dir="auto">This prints:</p>
<pre>Status: 200
Content-type: text/html; charset=utf-8
Body: &lt;!doctype html&gt; ...
</pre>
<p dir="auto">Coming from <a href="https://requests.readthedocs.io/" rel="nofollow">requests</a> ? Read <a href="https://aiohttp.readthedocs.io/en/latest/http_request_lifecycle.html" rel="nofollow">why we need so many lines</a>.</p>
<a name="user-content-server"></a>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Server</h3><a id="user-content-server" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Server" href="#server"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">An example using a simple server:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto"><pre><span class="pl-c"># examples/server_simple.py</span>
<span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s1">aiohttp</span> <span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-s1">web</span>

<span class="pl-k">async</span> <span class="pl-k">def</span> <span class="pl-en">handle</span>(<span class="pl-s1">request</span>):
    <span class="pl-s1">name</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s1">request</span>.<span class="pl-c1">match_info</span>.<span class="pl-c1">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">'name'</span>, <span class="pl-s">"Anonymous"</span>)
    <span class="pl-s1">text</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s">"Hello, "</span> <span class="pl-c1">+</span> <span class="pl-s1">name</span>
    <span class="pl-k">return</span> <span class="pl-s1">web</span>.<span class="pl-c1">Response</span>(<span class="pl-s1">text</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s1">text</span>)

<span class="pl-k">async</span> <span class="pl-k">def</span> <span class="pl-en">wshandle</span>(<span class="pl-s1">request</span>):
    <span class="pl-s1">ws</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s1">web</span>.<span class="pl-c1">WebSocketResponse</span>()
    <span class="pl-k">await</span> <span class="pl-s1">ws</span>.<span class="pl-c1">prepare</span>(<span class="pl-s1">request</span>)

    <span class="pl-k">async</span> <span class="pl-k">for</span> <span class="pl-s1">msg</span> <span class="pl-c1">in</span> <span class="pl-s1">ws</span>:
        <span class="pl-k">if</span> <span class="pl-s1">msg</span>.<span class="pl-c1">type</span> <span class="pl-c1">==</span> <span class="pl-s1">web</span>.<span class="pl-c1">WSMsgType</span>.<span class="pl-c1">text</span>:
            <span class="pl-k">await</span> <span class="pl-s1">ws</span>.<span class="pl-c1">send_str</span>(<span class="pl-s">"Hello, {}"</span>.<span class="pl-c1">format</span>(<span class="pl-s1">msg</span>.<span class="pl-c1">data</span>))
        <span class="pl-k">elif</span> <span class="pl-s1">msg</span>.<span class="pl-c1">type</span> <span class="pl-c1">==</span> <span class="pl-s1">web</span>.<span class="pl-c1">WSMsgType</span>.<span class="pl-c1">binary</span>:
            <span class="pl-k">await</span> <span class="pl-s1">ws</span>.<span class="pl-c1">send_bytes</span>(<span class="pl-s1">msg</span>.<span class="pl-c1">data</span>)
        <span class="pl-k">elif</span> <span class="pl-s1">msg</span>.<span class="pl-c1">type</span> <span class="pl-c1">==</span> <span class="pl-s1">web</span>.<span class="pl-c1">WSMsgType</span>.<span class="pl-c1">close</span>:
            <span class="pl-k">break</span>

    <span class="pl-k">return</span> <span class="pl-s1">ws</span>


<span class="pl-s1">app</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s1">web</span>.<span class="pl-c1">Application</span>()
<span class="pl-s1">app</span>.<span class="pl-c1">add_routes</span>([<span class="pl-s1">web</span>.<span class="pl-c1">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">'/'</span>, <span class="pl-s1">handle</span>),
                <span class="pl-s1">web</span>.<span class="pl-c1">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">'/echo'</span>, <span class="pl-s1">wshandle</span>),
                <span class="pl-s1">web</span>.<span class="pl-c1">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">'/{name}'</span>, <span class="pl-s1">handle</span>)])

<span class="pl-k">if</span> <span class="pl-s1">__name__</span> <span class="pl-c1">==</span> <span class="pl-s">'__main__'</span>:
    <span class="pl-s1">web</span>.<span class="pl-c1">run_app</span>(<span class="pl-s1">app</span>)</pre><div class="zeroclipboard-container">
    <clipboard-copy aria-label="Copy code to clipboard" class="ClipboardButton btn btn-invisible js-clipboard-copy m-2 p-0 d-flex flex-justify-center flex-items-center" data-copy-feedback="Copied!" data-tooltip-direction="w" value="# examples/server_simple.py
from aiohttp import web

async def handle(request):
    name = request.match_info.get('name', &quot;Anonymous&quot;)
    text = &quot;Hello, &quot; + name
    return web.Response(text=text)

async def wshandle(request):
    ws = web.WebSocketResponse()
    await ws.prepare(request)

    async for msg in ws:
        if msg.type == web.WSMsgType.text:
            await ws.send_str(&quot;Hello, {}&quot;.format(msg.data))
        elif msg.type == web.WSMsgType.binary:
            await ws.send_bytes(msg.data)
        elif msg.type == web.WSMsgType.close:
            break

    return ws


app = web.Application()
app.add_routes([web.get('/', handle),
                web.get('/echo', wshandle),
                web.get('/{name}', handle)])

if __name__ == '__main__':
    web.run_app(app)" tabindex="0" role="button">
      <svg aria-hidden="true" data-component="Octicon" height="16" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" data-view-component="true" class="octicon octicon-copy js-clipboard-copy-icon">
    <path d="M0 6.75C0 5.784.784 5 1.75 5h1.5a.75.75 0 0 1 0 1.5h-1.5a.25.25 0 0 0-.25.25v7.5c0 .138.112.25.25.25h7.5a.25.25 0 0 0 .25-.25v-1.5a.75.75 0 0 1 1.5 0v1.5A1.75 1.75 0 0 1 9.25 16h-7.5A1.75 1.75 0 0 1 0 14.25Z"></path><path d="M5 1.75C5 .784 5.784 0 6.75 0h7.5C15.216 0 16 .784 16 1.75v7.5A1.75 1.75 0 0 1 14.25 11h-7.5A1.75 1.75 0 0 1 5 9.25Zm1.75-.25a.25.25 0 0 0-.25.25v7.5c0 .138.112.25.25.25h7.5a.25.25 0 0 0 .25-.25v-7.5a.25.25 0 0 0-.25-.25Z"></path>
</svg>
      <svg aria-hidden="true" data-component="Octicon" height="16" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" data-view-component="true" class="octicon octicon-check js-clipboard-check-icon color-fg-success d-none">
    <path d="M13.78 4.22a.75.75 0 0 1 0 1.06l-7.25 7.25a.75.75 0 0 1-1.06 0L2.22 9.28a.751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018L6 10.94l6.72-6.72a.75.75 0 0 1 1.06 0Z"></path>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Requirements</h2><a id="user-content-requirements" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Requirements" href="#requirements"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>

<li><a href="https://pypi.python.org/pypi/multidict" rel="nofollow">multidict</a></li>
